Delegated on the container (`mouseover` bubbles, `mouseenter` does not) plus an identity guard, which is #215's argument for `g.onmouseover` on `#bGrid` applied to a harder version of it. **Measured with a real pointer and a render counter: 1 render entering the row, 1 sliding card to card, 1 leaving, and 0 in the 1.5s after.** **20% SMALLER, AND `--fs0` IS A MEASUREMENT RATHER THAN AN EXEMPTION.** `.claude/rules/ui-scales.md` §1 has said since #164 that 10px is the FLOOR and that the answer to a shouting readout is *dimmer or narrower, never smaller* - and that rule is about what an EYE gets, while `#bGrid` is drawn inside a camera. Measured at all three stops: `.hodds` at `--fs1` renders **16.4 / 22 / 25 real px** and at `--fs0` **13.1 / 17.6 / 20**, so the smallest of them, at the stop that shows the most ground, is still three points over the floor. ⛔ **The probe in §5 moved with it** - it multiplies font-size by the element's own transform chain measured against `#stage`, so on every unscaled surface it reads exactly what it always read. **Proved by making it fire** (a seeded 9.5px `#buildTag` is reported, and 0 after restoring), and two of its own false positives were found and fixed on the way: a `display:none` SCREEN does not stop a child computing a normal display, and `matrix.a` alone reads **-1.00** on a mirrored element and 0 on the portrait rotation. `Math.hypot(a,b)` and an `offsetParent` guard: **0 on twelve screens.** **THE PARTING FIGURE STANDS UNDER THE BODY.** #213 put it on the swinger, #224 moved it to the hex the step is aimed at; both were hunting a slot nothing else was using, and both landed on a readout that MOVES with the pointer. The body being charged does not move. ⛔ **ONE FIGURE, AND IT IS THE CHANCE OF TAKING A HIT AT ALL**: three chances joined by dots is a list, and `1 - Π(1-p)` over the bodies holding you is the answer to the question the chip exists for - two holders at 61 and 40 read **77**, which is neither of them and is true. ⚠ A holder whose reach act carries no `dmg` shoves and cannot hurt you, so it is out of the product and still in the hover. ⛑ **The plate went with the moving**: #213 needed an opaque ground and a red edge to survive a body standing on the chip's hex, and this is text with the same triple halo the two rows above it wear. Captured at 3x on a lit board: legible over the mover's own ground, and the z6 lift keeps it clear of the row painted in front. ⚡ **`.claude/rules/ui-scales.md` §3's table is one row shorter as a result** - `padding:1px 2px` was this build's second geometry literal and the file's grep now returns `86px` alone. **THE FLAG.** #228 measured `left:50%` at 1.55 design px inside the token's right edge and wrote down that a fifth cut wider than Broken would overhang. `right:0` deletes the question: the flag's right edge IS the token's edge at every width. Widest cut, measured live: **16.5..31.45 -> 20.19..33.0** against a 33px token, i.e. **3.7px further right with 0 overhang by construction**, and 14px -> 12px is what paid for it. ⚠ The captain's star still never comes into it: 3px of vertical clearance, up from 1. **THE STAMP.** ⛔ **The bottom right corner is FULL and only a probe said so**, which is #226's own finding at the other end of the same column: `#bFlee` y654..688, `#reactBar` x1163..1242 y694..714, `#testBtn` x1248..1272 y692..714, and the widest clear band on that edge is **four pixels**. So it sits on the same bottom row, left of FEEDBACK, at `right:125px` - one rule for every screen instead of #226's three, measured off a button that ships in the player build too. Probed at x1133..1155 y704..714 against every visible box on **twelve screens**: clean on eleven, and on the sheet it lands on `#iStash`'s own empty corner with no child there. **THE CARD STOPS AT THE SKILLS.** ⛔ **The floor was a literal 300 and the card has never been 300** - the eleven bodies of the opening fight measure 312 to 336, so the clamp let a card's top reach 420 in a 720 field and its foot land at 756: 136px under the act row and 36 under the field. ⛑ Both numbers are read now, neither typed: `rd.offsetHeight` after `.on` (the box is `display:none` before it, so a measurement there is 0) and `#bActions`'s own top, which is bottom-anchored and shrinks to nothing on the enemy's turn, so the floor rises by itself. **The old line replayed against the new one on the same eleven bodies: 2 of 11 ran up to 68px under the row, now 0 of 11 with 8px of clearance.** The band is 604px and the tallest card is 336, so nothing needs to scroll - which matters, because `#bRead` is `pointer-events:none` and a scrollbar there is one nobody can reach. **WHAT IS ON IT** is deleted and its hairline and rows are not, the same deletion as #219's THE ROOM SETTLES. **Gates:** `LINT()` **0 findings** · the map's three counters **0 / 0 / 0** · the scale-aware floor **0 on twelve screens** · nothing newly clipped (`#bField`, `#bTrait`, `#bLog`, `#wMap`, `#menu` only, all on `ui-scales.md`'s known-and-pre-existing list) · `font-size` px literals **0** · padding literals now `86px` alone · structural edge hexes **0** · **no em dash added** · `__cssTail` true · a clean console · and click-to-swing under a standing MOVE still spends the action. | ✅ DONE |

Full skill, full dodge.* · *9 off their chance to hit, 4 off their dodge.* · *12 off..., 5 off...* ⚠ **Broken is his own sentence with one clause kept**: he wrote *"Are running from the map and will not participate in the battle"* and RALLY still exists, so it ships as *Routed. Running off the map and out of the battle, unless somebody rallies them* - **a readout may not promise a rule the fight does not keep**, which is the same law that governs a door's receipt. **AND UNDER IT, WHAT ACTUALLY MOVES THE POOL**, three lines, every figure read off `MORALE{}` rather than typed: *an enemy died somewhere +10 · standing among allies · they killed somebody +30* against *an ally goes down -14 · surrounded by enemies · taking a blow -5*. ⚑ **The two crowd rows carry no figure on purpose**: they are weighted by `nearWeight` and a single number there would be the lie. **THE BANNER IS ONE RUNG, NOT TWO.** #228 gave `rock` to It rocks AND to Happy, and Happy is where every body on the field starts - so the picture that means *this one is flying* was the default state of the company. `FLAG_BY_RUNG[1]` is `null`; `nerveFlag` already returned '' on a keyless rung, so it was one word. ⚠ `FLAG_SHOWN` is indices off the ENDS and is untouched: rung 1 was never drawn on the board, so the battlefield reads byte-for-byte as it did. **THE CAPTAIN WEARS THE BOARD'S STAR AND A TRAIT WRITTEN FOR HIM.** `CLASS_ICON.captain` is `★`, which is what `#bName` and the crest have printed since #96. **SLEEPS IN THE HARNESS**: +1 STR, +1 MOR, and **-2 MOR in anything under HEAVY**, to his spec. ⛔ **The condition lands in `effStats` beside `sizeMismatch` and nowhere else** - that function is where every modifier on this build already meets, and the ill-fitting-armour rule is the same shape (a gear fact that costs MOR). ⚠ **`bandKey` and its two thresholds moved ~6,800 lines UP to sit above `effStats`**, because a `const` read from above its own declaration is the temporal-dead-zone throw that aborts the script and this repo has shipped it once; `ARMOUR_BANDS` itself did not move. Moved into that hole `.idrv` costs **nothing** (the column is still shorter than the tells) and `#iStats` loses a row and a gap: **31px back**. ⚠ `.ibars` went **200 -> 214** to buy the wrap its second line - at 200 the Captain's line fell 76+128 and went to four - and the tells gave the 14 up and still measure **222 into 245**. ⛔ **AND THE ICON COLUMN WAS NEVER AN ICON COLUMN.** `.itl .tvw` was `justify-content:flex-end` in a fixed-width row, so **the length of the WORD decided where the PICTURE began**: four rows, four left edges, **46px apart on the mage**, measured. `flex-start` plus a fixed 78px label column (INTELLECT is 73 at `--fs2`, canvas-probed) puts all four icons on **567** and all four words on **603** for every body in the party. ⚠ The row is `align-items:center` too: a 32px painting baselined against a 10px label hangs its whole body below the row it belongs to, which is the other half of what he was describing. **AND THE SKILL CARD PUTS THE DAMAGE WHERE THE BATTLE PUTS IT.** #133 built `.iact` as "`.act` re-cut" and then moved the one element `.act` keeps in its flow into an absolute top-right corner, where it sat **on** the icon. In flow, `.iact` 78 -> **90**, which is `.act`'s own height. **THE BODY**'s caption is deleted (the picture explains itself; #140 had already cut it to two words on that argument) and **WHAT THEY CAN DO** is **SKILLS** - ⚠ the battle hover keeps its sentence, because there it alternates with WHAT IT CAN DO TO YOU and the pair is the readout. **The field token left the portrait tile** for the right end of the header, where the header's third column was empty on every body in the game. **And the short sword left the starting stash**, which overrules #161's argument on his own call: the first inventory the player opens is one row again. **WHAT IT ALL COST, MEASURED ON A CREW MEMBER (the worst case for HEIGHT, #230):** slack **14.6 -> 32.4**, and the Captain **37 -> 70.4**. **Gates:** `LINT()` **0 findings** · map **0 / 0 / 0** · the scale-aware floor **0 on the sheet, the map, the battle and the reference**, and **proved by making it fire** · the clip counter reports only `#wMap`, `#bField`, `#bLog` (all on ui-scales.md's known-and-pre-existing list) · `#iChar`'s paint-over probe **[] on all four bodies** · `font-size` px literals **0** · padding literals **`86px` alone** (the `.moodwhy` rule shipped a 5px and was caught by the file's own grep) · structural edge hexes **0** · no em dash added. | ✅ DONE |
| 8f.256 | **#233 - THE NEW WORLD MAP IS CANON AND IT IS THE FIRST ONE DRAWN AT THE CANVAS'S OWN RESOLUTION, AND SEVEN PORTRAITS ARE REPAINTED.** *(User, 2026-08-22: "there new global map - take it as canon and then deploy", then "and portraits: art/src/stage-2/portrait-refresh-2026-08-22".)* ⛑ **THE MAP IS 2560x1276 AND THAT NUMBER IS THE WHOLE ENTRY.** #223 measured the blur complaint and wrote down that it could not be answered: *"the three world-map sources are 1280x638, which is exactly the design box - so at the NEAR camera stop the painting is enlarged 1.22 and there is no more data anywhere ... the terrain itself is as sharp as a 1280-wide file gets and **only new art moves that number**."* This is that art. The canvas backing store has been 2x since #223, so the new master is a **1:1 pixel match to the store** and the NEAR stop is now a downscale instead of an upscale. ⚠ Same aspect ratio to four places (2.0063), so nothing is stretched and no node moved. ⚑ **`build_assets.ps1` needed no change**: the world-map path has always embedded the chosen file **verbatim**, and it reads which key to take **out of the prototype**, so a 4x-larger source simply goes in whole. 307 KB -> 1246 KB embedded. **AND THE DIM WAS RE-MEASURED RATHER THAN ASSUMED.** `WORLD_MAP_DIM` was tuned by #223 against the OLD painting; the new one under the same filter reads **p10 22 / median 46 / p90 84, spread 62** (51,200 samples on an 8px grid off the live canvas) against that entry's recorded **27 / 61 / 96, spread 69**. ⛑ **That is darker at every quantile and it is the right direction**: #190's name plates were chosen against the procedural ground at **median 48**, and the painted map has been sitting 13 points brighter than the thing the type was designed for ever since. The filter is unchanged, and it suits this painting better than it suited the last one. ⚠ **What is NOT measured is business**: this is a far more detailed painting and the plates now sit over trees and rock rather than over a wash, which is a different question from luminance and is left open for his eye. **AND SEVEN PORTRAITS, INTEGRATED TO THEIR OWN HANDOFF.** `art/src/stage-2/portrait-refresh-2026-08-22/CLAUDE_INSTRUCTIONS.md` shipped with hard guards and every one was checked rather than trusted: the seven PNGs copied, the Markdown not; **POR-07, POR-08, POR-10 and POR-R05 byte-identical after the rebuild** (md5-compared before and after - Asha was named in the brief as untouchable); all seven outputs **exactly 220x220**. ⛑ **AND THE EMBED WAS PROVED AGAINST THE FILE, NOT AGAINST THE LOG**, which is #230's rule on a second surface: every one of the eleven `POR*` keys and the map key decoded out of the prototype and byte-compared to the file on disk it claims to be. **12 of 12 MATCH.** **Gates:** map counters **0 / 0 / 0** · the road-through-a-painting check **[]** · `LINT()` 0 · seven faces still distinct at the 48px roster bust and each identity detail from the handoff table legible at 220 (contact sheet at the three sizes the game actually draws). | ✅ DONE |
| 8f.257 | **#234 - THE BOARD ANSWERS AGAIN, THE BLOW IS DRAWN ON THE BAR IT COMES OUT OF, AND THE COMPANY SHEET'S HEADER PAYS FOR ITS OWN AIR.** *(User, 2026-08-22, seventeen asks in two messages, opening with a screenshot with two red arrows on a target's health bar.)* **THE BOARD (asks 1-6, 12).** #231 gated the hit % on `pickA||hovA` because *"an answer with no question in front of it is furniture"*; the ask overrules it in its own parenthesis - *"enemies who are in range of my attack (without movment)"* - and that question IS standing all turn. The gate is `offA`, i.e. #91's fall-through is back inside it, and nothing else moved: `udist(u,occ)<=range` is measured from the hex the body stands on, so a foe you would have to cross the board to touch never carried a figure and still does not. ⛑ **THE DAMAGE IS NOW DRAWN ON THE TWO BARS IT ACTUALLY COMES OUT OF**, `dp.hp` on the red row and `dp.arm` on the grey one, built on every in-reach foe and shown only on the hex the click would land on - `.hex.hand .ubars b.dpv`, the same one-body gate `.hdmg` has had since #231. It is not a second receipt: the figure over the hex is *how hard this swing hits* (`full`, armour ignored, #98 point 7, for comparing two CARDS) and the band is *what it takes off THIS body*. ⛔ **AND IT LIGHTS THE DOOMED RUN RATHER THAN DARKENING IT, WHICH THE FIRST CUT GOT WRONG AND ONLY THE PICTURE SAID.** The track under an empty bar is `#2a1e1f`, so dark already means *gone* on that element; a near-black wash read as a bar that was already that short. A warm lift at `rgba(255,196,150,.45)` under a lit left edge is a mark nothing else on the bar wears, and one wash reads on all nine lightnesses `hpCol` can return AND on the pale grey shell. The banner went `right:0` -> `right:-3px` (measured on a live board: its right edge moved from 2px inside the hex to 1px past it, +3 exactly, and the overhang stays a constant at every cut because the anchor is an edge and not a percentage). **SHAKEN FLIES AGAIN**, at .34 against Breaking's .55 and Broken's 1 - three steps, which is the ask's own *"more transparent, then broken"*, and it buys back the half of #227's subtraction that was costing numbers (-9 to hit) while leaving Ok silent. **The hand on you now BREATHES while a step is being weighed**: `zocpulse`, which is `.usp.now`'s ivory `actorpulse` in red at the same 1.1s, gated on `#bGrid.aiming` - the identical test the parting-swing figure rides, so the swinger and its price light up together or not at all. ⚑ **ASK 6 WAS ALREADY TRUE AND IS NOW MEASURED**: on a foe's turn the grid reports `aiming` false, 0 `.zodds`, 0 `.hex.zhit`, 0 shown `.hodds`, 0 shown bands; mid-walk (`B.busy`) the same. `paintHand()` runs at the foot of `render()`, which is why none of it can go stale. **A verb that MOVES a body now says its own name over the body that swung it** (`verbFx`, `a.push||a.pull` only): the target said `SHOVED` and the chevrons said which way, and nothing said who or with what - on your own turn you pressed the card, on theirs the board simply slid. Scoped to three acts and not to `spend()` at large, or every swing in the game would print ARMING SWORD over itself. **THE TABLES (asks 7, 8, 11, 15, 16).** The chase's one ratkin puts the DOCK HOOK down for a SHORT KNIFE - #210's argument for the verb was right and is overruled by where the fight sits, second in the game, where a reach-2 puller is the only thing on the road that undoes the player's arrangement after they have made it; the Snare's hook-pole is untouched, so the verb is met once, later, on a card that is about being pulled apart. **Skree carries the bow** - #201 wrote the bill out (*"SO THE RATKIN DOOR LOSES ITS BOW"*) and its art argument was always about ASHA, so the bow goes back on the one whose painting can hold it: three fields, no art, because `paintedSpriteKey` has had a `ratkin_you_archer` branch keyed on `cls` since it was written (verified: he resolves to that key, and to `ratkin_you_cutter` when the class is put back). **The brawl's fifth man holds the doorway for the round he arrives on** (`holdTill`, a round number on the unit, guarded by `!engaged(u)` so walking up to him still starts a fight) - #145 split that wave in two so each round would carry one lesson, and then sent the knife in on the same round as the ogre. **The Captain's trait is `Experienced`, +1 STR and +1 MOR, and the heavy-armour condition is gone** at the user's call; `authored:true` is the second sanctioned exemption beside `foeOnly`, because a line nobody can ever roll cannot unbalance a pool it is not in (`CLS_BY_RACE` has never contained `captain`, so that was already true and nothing said so). **CUT A GEM is hidden with an empty pouch**, overruling #186's *"shown greyed"*: #156's built-and-invisible shape is about a feature you cannot find, and this one is found the moment a gem is picked up. **THE SHEET (asks 9, 13, 14, 17, 18).** ⛔ **THE HEADER PAID FOR ITSELF AND THAT IS THE WHOLE ENTRY.** `#iChar` measures 666px of content box and the worst body on the roster had **3.8px** of slack in it, so a taller portrait was arithmetically impossible until something left. `ON THE FIELD` was the tallest thing in the row and nobody had measured it: `writing-mode:vertical-rl` at `--fs1` is **85.2px** against the 58px token it labelled, i.e. the row was 95.2 high to hold a caption explaining a picture that is already unambiguous. The gem door gave 18.6, the caption 18.6, the portrait tile 5 against 14 spent. **Every artifact name now wears its tier's colour** through one `rarityName()` at five surfaces (worn slot, stash row, shop rack, hover tip, the aftermath's find) - the cheap half of #197's *"what is still not drawn is the frame"*, and most of it. **The three weakest BRAINS come down** to 18/21/24 wide from a flat 30: this family normalises on HEIGHT and the width cap caught all four lumps, so the four WEAKEST pictures came out the four WIDEST - grade 3 at 30x21 is the pair of round grey lobes the ask is describing. Grade 1 came down uncalled-for because the ladder was about to run big-small-small-big. **Gates:** `LINT()` **0** · font floor **0** on the sheet and the battle · clip counter identical to HEAD (`#bField`/`#bTrait`/`#bLog`, all four documented) · the #230 column-overlap probe **[]** · map counters **0/0/0** · file greps: 0 font literals, `86px` alone, 0 flattened edges · every card gate unchanged · **all 9 `STAT_ICON.int` rows byte-compared to the pack on disk, 9 of 9 MATCH** (which proves the three new pictures landed in the right rows AND that the exporter reproduces the six untouched ones identically) · the chase re-measured n=40 a side: rounds unchanged, bodies down 0.45 -> 0.25 mean and 38% -> 23% of runs, which is the pull being gone and is where the ask aimed · the eight-fight regression clean both sides, no ERR, no FATAL, no guard hit. ⛑ **AND THE SESSION'S SECOND HALF WAS THE USER ASKING WHY IT HAD TAKEN SO LONG** (*"что за файл py и зачем ты их делаешь?"*, *"как можно работать быстрее?"*), which produced **`tools/dev/`, the session toolkit, and the `/drive` skill**. | 8f.258 | **#235 - THE WORKING FILE STOPS CARRYING 27 MB OF BASE64: 30.2 MB -> 3.4 MB.** *(User, 2026-08-22, on being shown the arithmetic and asking the two questions that decide it: ‘would it be working when i upload it to the git? And will it be still easy to make build for itchi and similar?’)* **BOTH ANSWERS ARE YES AND NEITHER IS A PROMISE: NOTHING ABOUT WHAT SHIPS CHANGES.** GitHub Pages serves `index.html`, the itch upload is a zip with `index.html` at its root, and both are GENERATED by `tools/build_site.ps1` from the working file - which already poured the audio into a marker and now pours the art the same way. Measured on the built page: **350 `data:image` payloads, identical to the shipped `index.html`**, and it boots with every art table at its old count (ART 79 · JSTAGE_ART 65 · ITEM_ART 56 · BATTLE_ART 53 · MAP_ART 35 · INTENT_ART 8 · RACE_ART 3). `build_itch.ps1` calls `build_site.ps1 -Player` and inherits it for free. **THE CHANGE.** The two generated blocks - 12.96 MB of `__ART_DATA__` and 13.85 MB of `__J_PACK__`, **88% of the file** - are `art/embed/art_data.js` and `art/embed/j_pack.js`, tracked, loaded by two classic script tags. ⛑ **NOT ONE LINE OF GAME CODE CHANGED, AND THE REASON IS A LANGUAGE RULE: a top-level `const` in a CLASSIC script is a global lexical binding**, so `ART` and the rest are visible to the script below exactly as they were when they sat inside it. ⛔ Which is also why `defer` and `type=module` are forbidden on those two tags - either would drop the game silently to `HASART`'s procedural fallback. ⛑ **AND THAT FALLBACK IS WHY A MISSING FILE IS A DEGRADED PICTURE RATHER THAN A CRASH**: `HASART` has been `typeof ART!=='undefined'` since it was written. **WHAT IT RETIRES.** `inject.ps1` and `inject_j_pack.ps1` each read the whole 30 MB prototype and wrote it whole back, and each carried its own warning that this *“is the exact shape that erases a parallel session's work. Ask first.”* They forward to `art/embed.ps1` now and do not touch the prototype at all, so the `claim.ps1` gate goes with the hazard, and #209's careful reason for a SECOND injector (*“two desks that both run it produce the conflict nothing can resolve”*) becomes structural: two blocks, two files, nothing to collide over. `docs/PARALLEL_SESSIONS.md` item 2 was *“solved by isolation”* and is solved outright. ⚠ **`art/embed/` IS TRACKED AND MUST STAY TRACKED** - it is the only copy of the paintings in git now, and a desk is a fresh checkout, so an ignored `art/embed/` would hand every new desk a game with no art and no error saying why. ⛔ **TWO SELF-INFLICTED BUGS ON THE FIRST CUT AND THEY WERE THE SAME SHAPE: SYNTAX INSIDE A COMMENT THAT ENDS THE COMMENT.** The generated header named its marker as `/*__ART_DATA__*/` inside a `/* */` block, and the nested terminator closed it 12 MB early; and the HTML comment introducing the tags contained a literal script tag, so the inline-script scanner matched THAT and tried to parse English as JavaScript (*“Unexpected identifier 'the'”*). Both were named in under a second by a gate, neither by the browser. ⛑ **SO THE GATE GREW TO FIT: `gt.py check` FOLLOWS `<script src>` NOW AND STRIPS HTML COMMENTS FIRST.** A checker that only read inline scripts would have gone on printing *0 errors* over a build whose paintings did not parse - it did, for one round. **Proved by making it fire**: a deliberate `const oops = ;` appended to `j_pack.js` reports and exits 1; restored, 3 scripts, 0 problems. **Gates:** `LINT()` 0 · both ui-scales counters clean on the sheet and the battle · the #230 overlap probe [] · map 0/0/0 with 34 sights, 0 orphans and a terrain canvas with real pixels in it · the eight fights with no ERR, no FATAL, no guard · the built page 350/350 and booting. | ✅ DONE |

It returns what it paid, so `pickChoice`'s chip, `clashAfter`'s travelling haul and `consequences`' chip row all report the payment; `evPaidOf` takes the same cut so `fxNote` promises it. **Measured on the same roads, before -> after: a salvage-taking company holds 18-21 -> 10-12 at the Black Fen and 42 -> 23-24 at the last fire; greediest-door 45 -> 25 and 69-84 -> 39-46.** The wagon's 6/12/24 is deliberately NOT re-priced (the user's call): tier III is now a greedy-run purchase and tier II a late one. **EXPERIENCE IS EARNED A FIFTH SLOWER** through `XP_RATE` at `grantXP`, which is the one door every grant passes - scaling `XP_PER_HP` alone would have left the road's `xp:` doors, the camps and the vignettes at full rate. On the EARNED side and not the NEEDED side because `XP_TABLE` is a triangle of round hundreds a player reads on a hover. **A CUT GEM PAYS A LEVEL'S WORTH OF EXPERIENCE INSTEAD OF THE REMAINDER**: the old pair (`levelUp` then floor `xp` to the threshold) punished progress, so the best moment to spend the once-a-run gem was on somebody who had just levelled. It goes through `grantXP(..., raw)` - a purchase is not earnings and does not pay the 20%. Driven: 60% into level 2 -> level 3 at 48%, one level exactly. **AND A LEVEL IS NOW WORTH SOMETHING BEFORE A POINT IS SPENT**: +5% hitpoints, +1 to hit, +1 dodge per step above the first (Vesna 57/69/15 at L1 -> 66/72/18 at L4 -> 77/76/22 at L8). ⛑ **That forced `bodyHp`, and it found a shipped lie**: the field, the company sheet and the paper doll were computing hitpoints THREE ways, and the sheet printed the bare stat curve - so an archer's sheet said **50** while his battle plaque said **45**. One door now; Marrow reads 48 and Ilka 45 on both. **THE PRISONERS ARE WORTH WHAT IS ACTUALLY KNEELING THERE.** He named the chase; the fault was in every fight. The three rows paid a flat +30/+5 and +60/+9/+1 gem whether one body was begging or six, so the chase - one ratling, node three - handed over a gem. Per head off `MERCYASK`, rates fitted to the figures they replace so a big rout is not a payday nobody asked for: **1 prisoner +8/+1 and +15/+2, no gem; 4 prisoners +32/+2 and +60/+4/+1 gem**. Morale stays flat because what it costs to watch you do this is a fact about the DECISION. **"THEY ARE ALL RUNNING" WAITS A WHOLE ROUND.** `every(routed)` is true for an instant on fields that are not broken, and the card is a DOOR that ends the fight. `B.routSince` is the sentence "they have all been running since last round", cleared the moment anybody rallies, and stepped outside the `!routAsked` guard so the clock runs on turns the question cannot be asked. Driven on a beast fight (a field of PEOPLE never reaches this branch): clock starts at round 5, no offer; same round, no offer; round 6, offer; a rally clears it and it must earn the round again. **A BEAST STOPS WEARING A FACE UNLESS IT IS THE BOSS** (`monBoss`, a `boss:` field on two MONSTERS rows, not a list of keys): a dog's rail reads `PACK-BEAST` and its token badge is empty; the Fen-Mother keeps `🐲`. The class, the tag and `u.monster` are untouched - only the pictogram goes. **AND A BODY IS DRAWN HOLDING WHAT IT IS HOLDING**: `wepShape` is the paper doll's own range/reach/hands cascade lifted out, `unitFrom` puts it on the unit, and `paintedSpriteKey` reads it for everybody except the three the user excluded - the Captain, Asha and a caster, all of whom are already tested above the weapon. An ogre spearwoman handed a warclub draws `ogre_you_brute`; a ratkin cutter handed a bow draws `ratkin_you_archer`. **THE TAP-ROOM: THE BARMAN COMES IN ALONE.** No knife that round, no crew the next. `tavernWave3` keeps its body and loses its caller; `TAVERN_T.weft` and `TUTSTEPS.crew` stay on the register. ⛔ **`checkEnd`'s wave net had to change in the same edit or the brawl soft-locks** - #205's note there says wave two may `return false` on an empty field *because it puts a knife on it*, and with the knife gone it is allies only. **n=50 a side: the Captain finishes at 39.6% against HEAD's 39.0, 7.5 rounds against 7.4; AUTO-driven losses 7 against 4, which n=50 cannot separate from noise and is the number to watch.** **THE SHOPS ROLL THEIR OWN STOCK**, one common that is not a main-hand weapon, one rare, one epic, off `findPool` - the one pool, which already refuses uniques, wrong-sized armour, what you own, `earlyFind` and a weapon that is not an advance. That needed a price for all 54 `GEAR` rows where twelve had one, so `gearPrice` is FITTED to those twelve and every one lands within a sixth: jack 100 (95), mail 200 (210), plate 290 (260), shortsw 65 (65), spear 75 (75), bow 105 (110), crossbow 175 (165), buckler 50 (55), quiver 50 (50). `QM_RACK` and `ARMOURY` are gone; their prices survive as the calibration in the function's header. **Coldharrow buys in ONE CLICK** - its second screen was literally the same row again, one behind the other. ⛔ **Three pictured rows is +322px on a card with a hard `max-height:658` and `overflow-y:hidden`: measured, it overflowed by 165 and `#wChoices` is sticky, so the buttons won and `.bd` was crushed to ZERO - the whole Coldharrow paragraph on the card and invisible.** The 212px painting comes off (#238's own ruling on the wagon, one card later) and the card measures **0 overflow**, prose 77px, every row inside the map. ⚑ That painting was `artFor('E5')`, the retired first-generation SNARE plate: `EVENTART.snare` was re-pointed at `EV27` by the stage-4 pack, so Coldharrow had been showing a painting of somewhere else that no card maps to. **And a shop row says what the thing is** - `TIER · SLOT · TWO HANDS`, `tipFor`'s own scope line, in the stash row's own mono label. **THE SHEET.** The road bar's two readouts MOVE onto `#iBar` when the sheet opens and go home in `#wBar`'s own order (the `helpBtn` reparent, twelve lines up); a second 42px bar is arithmetically closed, because `#iChar` is 678 into 678. | 8f.263 | **#240 - THE NINETEEN-ASK BATCH: THE RING STOPS HELPING ARROWS, A DEATH IS A FRACTION OF THE LINE, THE SLING-LINE ACTUALLY HOLDS, AND THE WAGON LEAVES THE MENU.** *(User, 2026-08-24, nineteen asks in one message, the second batch of the day, grouped by screen the way #239's twenty were.)* **TWO OF THE NINETEEN WERE REPORTS ABOUT SOMETHING THE BUILD WAS NOT DOING AND BOTH TURNED ON THE SAME WORD, "already".** *"стебель цветка - только под ивент с блумом (ка щас)"* describes a state the build did not have: fourteen `GEAR` keys are handed over by a card in `EVENTS` or `CAMPS`, **four carried `unique` and ten did not**, so the Bloom-Stem, the Pedlar's Charm and the fiddler's Rosined String could all be rolled into a battlefield find or (since #239) a shop rack. It is still a price and not a ban: added to every ember hex equally, it cancels when the fire is the only ground that reaches anybody. **THE OGRE THROWS ONE OF YOURS AT SOMEBODY** - `throwSpots` has carried an `aimed` flag since #46 and the BOULDER passes it; a body was the one cargo that could only be put on empty ground. It cannot miss because `act.impact` already exists and already prints *"movement becomes the hit"*. ON THE SACK IS AN EVENT AND NOT A STANDING TRUTH**: `kitWaiting()` asks *is anything in the cart better than what somebody wears*, which stays true once true, so the badge was on for most of the run. `stashGain` is the one door kit arrives through FROM THE WORLD - the ten sites that are a delivery, never the six that are a body handing its own sword back. **TWO SWITCHES**: `AU.on` keeps `gt_mute` and is the effects; `MUS.on` is new. ⚠ On the board the pair is a COLUMN, and that is a measurement: side by side the box is 96px against one button's 46 and it sat over **2 playable hexes**; `column-reverse` from `top:50` keeps ♪ at exactly the 74 its own comment records and takes the 51px of nothing above it. **THE HOVER CARD STOPS BEING FLOORED BY A ROW IT IS NOT OVER** - `#bActions` is 335px of the field's 1280, so #231's floor was squeezing every card into the band above a row it usually stands clear of. So **FULL is pixel-identical to HEAD** - grid at 157.9,70.9, 964.3x682.2, `pans` false, the same four controls over the same 26 hexes - and #206's arithmetic about that stop is untouched, while the two panning stops can now push a rank clear of the chrome. **Measured, hexes under floating chrome with the acting body driven to each rim, HEAD to new: FIELD top 54 to 47, bottom 47 to 25, left 53 to 35, right 52 to 44; CLOSE top 57 to 47, bottom 49 to 25, left 47 to 27, right 49 to 39.** At the bottom rim the skill cards go **10 to 0** and **9 to 0**, END TURN 3 to 0, and the gap between the last rank and the card band goes **-100 to +40.8** (FIELD) and **-100 to +60** (CLOSE), which is the ask itself as a number. **THE PAINTER'S ORIGIN DID NOT MOVE.** The context is translated back onto the play area's corner, so every coordinate below it - `hexCentre` included - still means what it meant, and the base gradient is unchanged with only its rect grown (a canvas gradient clamps to its end colours past its own ends), so the 588x426 inside is painted exactly as it was. **`apronDress` HAS ITS OWN SEEDED STREAM**, off `B.terrSeed`: `paintTerrain`'s `rnd` is ONE shared sequence in draw order, so scattering a single tuft with it would move every puddle, stone and reed on the board. Proved: three repaints identical, a re-seeded field different, the seed back and identical again. ⚠ A hash over the WHOLE canvas reports *unstable* for a reason that is not this - the play area's trees are images and every `onload` repaints. The rim shade is 0 at the field's own edge and `APRON_RIM` .42 at the outer rim, over the scatter, so the far band fades with the ground it stands on. **`APRON_LATTICE` SHIPS AT 0 AND IS THE WAY BACK**: the ask contains both *чуть выходящие квадратики* and *они не размечены*, and the second is the sentence about how it LOOKS. Give it an alpha and the apron's hexes are drawn as outlines under the same fade; **proved by making it fire at .10 and turning it back**. **AND THE CAMERA'S APRON IS READ OFF THE BOX (`apronOf`) AND NEVER ASSUMED**, so a build where `apronApply()` has not run - the harness, the arena, a stubbed render - measures 0 on all four sides and every line of that arithmetic collapses to what it was. `camNudge` and `camPans` take the same pair as `camApply` through one `camRest`, or two writers of one transform would disagree about which box they were moving and a drag would jump when the player let go. **Gates:** `LINT()` **0** · font floor `[]` · clip **identical to HEAD** (`#bField`/`#bTrait`/`#bLog`) · map **0/0/0** · no em dash, 0 doors without an intent, 1 `?` · the eight arena fights clean · `hexSpan` exactly 38x z at all three stops, so every effect radius is where it was · `camNudge` driven past both rims settles on exactly `[0,0]` and `[VW-w, VH-h]` and does not move at FULL. **Open:** **at FULL nothing moved and nothing can** - 588x416 at z1.64 is 964x682 in a 1280x720 window, so the cards still float over the bottom two ranks there and #206's *the chrome yields instead* is still the answer at that stop; the apron pays at FIELD and CLOSE · pushing to one rim shows more of the opposite side, so a couple of counts rise where others fall (the plaque 14 to 16 at the right rim) · the band carries grass and stones off the palette and nothing else: **the furniture the ask was really about (*всякие дизайн штуки ... для атмосферности мира*) is one function, `apronDress`**, and it is commented as the place. | ✅ DONE |

The clash runs BOTH sides (its cast depends on whose side you take), the tap-room's four later waves come off the hoisted TAVERN_ROOM plan, and the dedupe key is **name + price**, because the Hold's men stand in `hold` AND `snarejoin` and a bench does not offer one body twice - a shared name at a DIFFERENT price is two statblocks and both stay. ⛔ **THE REFIT UNDERNEATH: startBattle's and fightPts' twin fight-to-builder ternary chains became ONE table, `FOE_BUILD`** - the drift shape this record keeps merging out (choiceNote #150, raceDoorPick #159, applyHurt #197, payMat #239), forced into the open by the catalogue needing a third reader. ⚠ **LINT 9c was reading `String(startBattle)` for the chain and instantly reported four fights missing** - the check that existed to catch a silent fallback caught the refactor that removed its subject. It asks the table itself now (same fact, no source parsing) and was proved by deleting a key: fired, restored, 0. **Scripted extras sit out with the cast they belong to**: a hand-picked side suppresses the clash allies, the snare clan and the brigand turncoat, skips the clash's side question (its whole payload is which cast stands where, and both casts are sitting out), and **refuses the tap-room** - its waves are scripted around its own cast, and a hand-picked side under a script that spawns the shipped one is two fights in one room. Deployment is the player's own slot shape mirrored to the far side, cap 14. **THE PERK EDITOR**: a ⚙ button on every crew row opens all 26 perks as chips; dev picks override the level policy (`what you ticked is what stands`), the FIRST tick seeds from what the policy had granted so one tick adds to the standing build instead of deleting seven, and the override lives behind TEST.on in crewPerson, so a row saved with picks reads as a plain policy row the moment the ⚙ goes off. **THE ORDERS**: `ORDER_PROF` - brain (the empty profile, i.e. the full AI), aggressive and steady verbatim from the gauntlet (cross-comments both sides; the tool runs against any build its iframe loads, so it keeps its own literals), and **defensive, NEW and first-cut** ({cohere:3.5, flank:0, pile:0.6} - hold the knot, no flanking runs, no focus pile), to be measured in the gauntlet before its numbers are trusted. A brain picked for your side engages AUTO at round one - the autobattle is the tester tool that already existed, pointed at a profile. simFight resets both sides to AIP_FULL before applying, simRestore resets again after, so no practice brain can leak. ⚠ **AND THE ONE TABLE CAUGHT A REAL LEAK**: ARENA's reset literal lacked `doct`, so an arena profile that set doct:0 survived its own finally and dulled every fight after the run - `FULL=AIP_FULL` now. **THE BOARD BRUSH**: seven buttons on the tester stack (at 254, under ✎ TEXT), writing the terrain map's own words - field, marsh, rock, tree, wall, fire - so pathing, cover, wet aim and the ogre's boulder pull react with no new rule anywhere. `handHex` gives an armed brush the click BEFORE the mirror, because the fold is for the HAND and a folded brush would paint the wrong half of the board; a hex with a body on it refuses; painting is free and takes no action. **Gates, driven on the desk build:** LINT **0** · clip `[]` and font floor `[]` with both benches and the perk panel open · the catalogue at 53, **byte-stable across two builds and every row proved buildable** (⚠ the first cut ran all builders in ONE seeded stream, and the steading's rolled wall made the stone-thrower appear and vanish by stream position - seededly is PER RUN now, so the catalogue and the side-builder can never disagree) · **a hand-picked side stood up on all 14 fight kinds, 0 throws, 0 leaked allies** · dev OFF fields the shipped brigands, ignores saved perk picks, and draws not one dev element · paint tree->rock landed, marsh under a standing body refused · after simResult: profiles 1, brush null, B null. **Open:** `defensive` is authored, not measured - a gauntlet column for it is the natural next run · the brush speaks six words; bloom and ember grounds are painted palettes, not terr words, so they are not brushable yet · the sim card scrolls ~790px with both benches open, which the sticky footer carries but a played session should judge · orders are per SIDE (that is what AIP is); per-body orders would be a different system. | ✅ DONE |

Proved by making it fire - run against `git show d0358c5:` it reports **delta 114.00,32.00 on all nine sampled hexes** and on the fixed build 0.00,0.00 on all nine. ⚠ **The first cut of that probe re-derived the offset by hand and got it wrong by exactly one apron**, i.e. it reproduced the bug it was testing for and reported a clean build as broken; it reads `apronCv().ox/oy` now, which is the one number the element and the painter both use. ⚑ **SO THE CANVAS IS DERIVED END TO END, WHICH IS THE USER'S OWN "it should be softer kind of".** `apronCv()` returns where the bitmap's corner goes, how big it is and where drawing (0,0) sits inside it; `apronApply()` writes that onto `#bGround` as `--aprCX/CY/CW/CH` and the stylesheet only holds the pre-apron fallbacks, so the element and the bitmap cannot come to disagree about the origin the way two hand-written coordinate systems just did. **`APRON_BLEED` (48px) IS PAINT BEYOND THE PANNABLE BAND**: the camera clamps to the ground box, so the apron is as far as the view can travel and the bleed can only ever be seen in FULL's own slack - without it a two-hex left apron (76px) is 20px short of FULL's 96px and the dark surround shows as a strip down one side. **LESS LEFT, MORE RIGHT: `APRON` is `{t:1,b:2,l:2,r:4}`**, i.e. 76 / 152 against the old 114 / 114, and the ground box is 816x512 either way. ⚠ **It is the PANNABLE band that is asymmetric**: at FULL the play area is centred, so what shows either side there is decided by the window and not by these numbers - the asymmetry is what you get when you zoom in and push the camera, and moving the field itself at FULL is a separate decision with #206's arithmetic against it. **THE RIM READS HARDER AND OVER A FIXED DISTANCE**: `APRON_RIM` .42 to **.58**, ramped over `APRON_FADE` **84 board px** from the field's own edge and flat beyond it, so all four rims are the same thickness whatever the apron is - Driven into all four corners at all three stops: **0px of empty ground on every side**, which is the other half of that ask. **THE ZONE OF CONTROL IS THE OUTSIDE OF THE SET.** #171 wrote in capitals *"it stays an EDGE and never becomes a FILL"* and then wrote `edge='rgba(150,40,45,.44)'`, which `render()` hands to `hedge.style.background`, i.e. the hexagon's GROUND. `drawZoc` draws each hex's side only when the hex across it is not in the zone - **14 zone hexes, 44 segments of a possible 84**, one shape with no seam down the middle - in `#bGround` so the camera is not a question, at `rgba(193,93,85,.5)` non-scaling, louder while a step is being aimed. | 8f.268 | **#244 - THE SIXTEEN-ASK BATCH: THE ZONE OF CONTROL BECOMES THREE SHORT LINES, THE WHOLE STRIDE IS ON THE BOARD, AND A HURT BODY WEARS IT.** *(User, 2026-08-25, sixteen asks the day after #243.)* **⛔ THE ZONE OF CONTROL IS THE SIDE A HEX SHARES WITH THE BODY HOLDING IT.** #243 replaced a red wash with an outline round the whole set, and that was half an answer: an outline round three hexes is still a shape the size of three hexes, and the eye reads an AREA when the rule is about a DIRECTION. `drawZoc` now picks the side facing each CONTROLLER instead of the side facing away from the set - **14 held hexes go from 44 segments to 17**, a short arc hugging each body, pointing where it looks. ⛑ **And that paid for the other half of the same ask**: `.backer`, the 22px part-circle rotated to a body's facing and drawn UNDER a 33px sprite, is deleted (*"if we are doing proper hex border in front, we can delete this thing (i feel it is hardly readible for a new players)"*). Nothing is lost - `.hex.stabhere` still puts a ✦ on every reachable hex that lands in somebody's back, which answers *where do I stand* rather than being a picture to work it out from - and the `back` whisper was rewritten in the same edit rather than left naming a mark that is gone. **THE WHOLE STRIDE IS ON THE BOARD**: `moveBudget2` asks `moveBudget` what a SECOND move would buy (it is not `b-1` - the cutter's first-move hex goes too) and the hexes only that reach gets are washed at half the alpha. Measured: **34 near, 41 far-only, 0 of the far ones clickable, and the far half disappears the moment a body has one action left**. ⚠ The gate is `B.sel.move` and not a cleared `B.reach2`: eight places in the file clear `B.reach` and a ninth will be written by somebody who never read this line. **AND A PICKED CARD BLINKS THE ACTIONS IT WOULD TAKE** off the plaque's pips, at 1.9s so it is not mistaken for the two 1.1s rhythms already on that screen. **⛔ A HURT BODY WEARS IT, AND IT GOES INTO THE PICTURE RATHER THAN ON TOP OF IT.** The obvious build is an overlay masked to the sprite and it comes apart the first time anything moves - `.usp` lunges, recoils, sways, shudders, flips and rotates when it routs. `spriteHurt` composites specks onto a copy of the sprite with `source-atop`, which is a free mask (it keeps only pixels that land on something already painted), at three tiers off `woundTier`, cached per PICTURE so two lurchers at the same tier share one bitmap. ⚠ It is asynchronous - a painted sprite is a data URI and has to go through an `Image` - so the clean picture shows until the `onload` re-renders, the same shape as the terrain's own trees. Measured: a lurcher's src goes **3310 bytes clean to 3938 bloodied**, and the two lurchers on the board stop being the same picture. **⛔ THE DEV KIT BENCH: ANY ARTIFACT AND ANY SKILL ONTO ANY BODY, MID-FIGHT.** It is the board brush's own shape pointed at bodies - arm, click, it lands, same `handHex` interception, same authority-hands-back when the ⚙ goes off. **55 artifacts and 54 skills**, and the skill list is DERIVED: every working in `SPELLS`, every race skill, and every act carried by every body every `FOE_BUILD` builder can field, through the same `seededly`/`devFoeUnits` pair #242's foe catalogue uses so building the list cannot move a rolled wall in the fight behind it. ⚠ An act is JSON-copied, never shared, because `cool` counts down on the object. ⚠ Gear goes through the ROSTER and then through `unitFrom`, because a weapon rewrites cards, reach, damage, the armour ceiling and whether a body is a shooter - and `KIT_KEEP` copies the DERIVED half back over the live one while where it stands and what it has left stay where they are. Driven: a maul onto a body at 24/60 hitpoints swapped the card and kept the hitpoints and the hex. **⛑ TWO ASKS WERE READ AS BUGS AND WERE NOT.** *"i still don't see how mage morale decrease after magic use"*: the game WAS charging him - Marrow's WILL-KICK measured morale 58 to 53 and the nerve bar 71.6% to 65.4% - and it was **saying it twice**, `mor()`'s own `◠▼▼` float beside `payStrain`'s `−5 mood`, both small, both under a spell's flight. One float now, full size, in the caster's pink, and the plaque's MOOD row flashes. *"Mood has option to normailise on global map too ... 1 per day for first distance, 2 per day second"*: **that is exactly what #161 built and nothing on screen had ever said so** - driven from 70 / 30 / -30 / -70 it walks 2/1/1/2 a day, and the hover names the rate now, derived off the same arithmetic. **THE REST, BY SCREEN.** Your side opens a fight at **`START_NERVE_YOU` .71 against the foes' .78** (#243 gave HIGH SPIRITS +15 and a fed company was opening on `It rocks`; measured, the Captain goes 75 to 68 and even at HIGH SPIRITS opens on Happy) · the off-hand dirk is **+5 to hit and +5 damage behind a MELEE weapon and a card behind a BOW**, never both (68->73 and 3->8 with a sword, unchanged with a bow, which gets the card) · `ARMOUR NONE` when there is nothing on · the kill tally is `3 KILLED` shut and the race heads only when the accordion is open · a banked stat point says `+2 TO A STAT · PICK A ROW` over the four rows it is spent on · an empty off hand under a two-hander opens no hover at all · `Let them go.` **Gates:** `LINT()` **0** · font floor `[]` on the sheet and the battle with both dev panels open · clip identical to HEAD · `iChar` overlap `[]` · map 0/0/0 · no em dash, 0 doors without an intent, 1 `?` · 0 literal `font-size` px, paddings still `0px 86px` · the eight arena fights clean over three sweeps · both dev panels measured over **0 playable hexes and no button**. **Open:** ⚠ **the dogs' pack bonus is live and correct and the ask could not be reproduced** - driven through a real SNAP-AND-AWAY the dog moves 2 hexes off your line and `aurasOn` recomputes on the spot, but **the Bitch's radius is 3 and the bounce is 2**, so a dog that bites and runs essentially never leaves her aura. That is the impression, and it is a dial (her radius, or the bounce) rather than a defect · the crossbow was already `hands:2` · `moveBudget2`'s preview maps a total budget in one pass, so it can show a hex whose only two-move route needs an illegal stop half-way; the far wash is deliberately not clickable · the kit bench writes onto the practice roster, which `simRestore`'s snapshot gives back, and is unreachable outside `SIM.on && TEST.on`. | ✅ DONE |
| 8f.269 | **#245 - THE ELEVEN-ASK BATCH: THREE THINGS WERE BUILT AND UNREACHABLE, AND ONE ASK WAS A REGRESSION REPORT.** *(User, 2026-08-25, eleven asks across two days.)* **⛔ THE MIREHARES HAD NEVER ONCE BEEN DEALT.** *"Where is rabits battle/event. Haven`t seen it in last 3 platesters runs"* - and nobody has. `f2` has carried `ev:'mirehares'` since #193 and `f2` was ALSO in `SLOTS_ON_MAP`, so `dealEvents()` overwrote the authored key at the top of every run: measured **0 survivals in 20 deals**, and `mirehares` is not in `FLOATING` either, so a booted game had **no node carrying the key at all**. The card, the fight, the arena field, the wide stage (`EVJ18`) and the map sight (`MAPEV34`) were all built and all unreachable. It is a fixed place now - **The Red Lights**, out of `SLOTS_ON_MAP` and out of `STASH_ARMS` (the cache would have deleted it a second way) - and **LINT 8h** asks the question nothing was asking: `MAP_SIGHT`'s own gate checks whether a key has ART and never whether a NODE can hold it. Proved by putting `f2` back in the slot list and watching it report. **It caught a second one on its first run**: `wynn` is painted and its card opens from inside the Snare, so no node could ever carry it - documented out of the table beside `cache` and `armour`. **⛔ THE BAG STOPS BEING A PROMISE, AND #192's ITEM PACK WAS THE SAME SHAPE.** *"I also have never seen a throvables and consumbles"*, then *"there was more consumbles, throwbles, but i also havent seeen them in the battle"*. He is right twice: #140 built the BAG slot deliberately empty (*"the day a potion exists it needs no code here"*), and #192 then wrote `art/ITEM_PACK_01_TEXT.md` with five items in `GEAR{}`'s own shape *"so the row can be pasted"*, painted all five, embedded them as `ITEM01..ITEM05` - and **pasted none of them**. Four bag items now: the **Healing Draught** (10 hitpoints, once a fight, `rare`, the ask's own numbers), the **Pilum** (range 3, 22-32, `am` 1.0, one throw), the **Shuriken** (range 4, two throws, a hit costs the target an action next turn) and the **Time-Cube** (every foe at half actions for two rounds, then it is gone for good). All five numbers are #192's, transcribed; the only substitution is UNCOMMON → `rare`, which that file's own ⚠ already made. One counter (`u.bagUsed`) holds *per battle* for every bag act and needs no clock, because `unitFrom` builds a new unit for each fight. **A throw needed no new engine**: `strike` already resolves `range`+`dmg`+`am`+`ft` and `RACESKILL.ogre` has been a range-4 act on a bowless body since #46. Driven end to end: the draught heals 10 and cannot overheal (42/42 from four short), the star slows to 1 and a second star REFRESHES rather than stacking, the foe's next turn is 1 action and the turn after is 2, the third star is refused, the cube sets `cubeUntil=round+2`, halves foes to 1, leaves a one-action body its one, leaves YOUR side at 2, expires, and deletes itself from `eq.bag`. ⚠ The slow is applied **after** `despActs`, not beside the handout: that branch is an assignment and would have erased it on exactly the bodies that are nearly dead. **`FIND_SLOT` learned the slot** - a bag item could previously only arrive through `rollFind`'s last-ditch no-slot fall-through, i.e. by accident. ⏳ The **Kris** and the **Stone Shield** are still not built and are neither throwable nor consumable: the kris is a main-hand weapon whose whole content is a BLEEDING status that does not exist. **⛔ THE ENFORCER FINALE HAD DRIFTED 26 POINTS THE WRONG WAY.** *"в останні битві з енфросерами ... то більше енфорсерсів"*. `holdHost`'s own note ends *"joining the clan must not therefore be the EASY way to end the act"*; measured at n=30 it was **53% against the honest finale's 27%**. The fight had not been touched - eight bodies of clan on your side have been getting better for two months while this line stood still. **The preference was a regression report.** +2 bills takes it to **40%**, which is the intended ranking. ⚡ **And say the honest thing about the champions: at n=30 they did not move the win rate** - the bills did all thirteen points, which is this function's own recorded dial. What the boards changed is the shape and a round of speed. n=15 could not separate any of these rows (it read the +2-bills row at 33% and its own superset at 60%). **⛔ A CHAMPION IS A MULTIPLIER, NOT A STATBLOCK.** *"чемпіонів ... сильніший як юніт десь на 50% по здоровю та броні та дамагу, і на 10% по потрапляння і на 5% по доджу"*. One word on a plan row (`[10,5,'bill',38,'champ']`) and five multipliers in `build()`; all five read as multipliers because that is the one reading that makes the sentence uniform. Measured: the Hold billman 40/44/[11,17]/61/14 becomes **59/65/[16,26]/67/15**. A drawn **epaulette** top right (CSS, so it collides with no glyph by construction), a warm rim on the sprite, and `▰ CHAMPION` on the readout. ⚡ **The screenshot caught what the DOM did not**: the first champion photographed was a Hold CORPORAL, which carries `captain:true`, so the tag was ranked behind `⚑ CHIEFTAIN` and the epaulette had nothing anywhere explaining it - the picture said a thing the words denied. The tag composes now. **⛔ A RINGED BODY IS NOT LEFT ALONE.** *"Якщо догі окружили ... кусали два рази за хід"*. *Bite twice* is not a second rule - a dog has two actions and SNAP costs one; the only reason it never bit twice is that the first bite teleported it out of its own reach. One condition on `bounce`, keyed on `surroundOn` (three, #157's measured threshold) so **the ⊛ SURROUNDED badge already on the board IS the warning**. Driven: 3 holders and the dog stands and keeps biting with 2 of 2 actions; 1 or 2 holders and it still leaves 3 hexes. The act's note is derived off `bounce` now, so all four dogs say the same thing and all four say the ringed half - two carried the sentence and two carried nothing. **THE REST, BY SCREEN.** `PROVISIONS` becomes **SUPPLIES** and the name of a resource stops being typed: **28 player-facing sites** spelled it out by hand, and `RES_NAME` is now the one table beside `RES_ICON` - `resName` / `resCaps` / `resTitle`, with `RES_WORDS`' glyph regexes BUILT from it and the old word kept as an alias so a card nobody swept still glyphs · **the days sit on the road**: `edgeCtl`/`edgePt`/`edgeTan` give the painter and the placer one curve (`labelSpot` was scoring against the straight chord with a hand-copied `-dy*0.07`), and a new pass ZERO offsets along the curve's own normal, offset-outer so *near the line* beats *near the middle* - **mean 62 to 37 design px, 29 of 30 chips over 30px to 12**, and eight of the twelve have no clean box within 44px of their line anywhere · **the muster and the market open as often as you like** while you stand in them, keyed on the two events `openEvent` hands to a dedicated screen (the first cut keyed on `n.t` and `t:'shop'` is a DEALT type: re-opening the pedlar would have paid its loot twice) · **the zone of control answers when the hex is asked** - `zocNote` through `groundNote`'s two existing renderers, so the empty hex and the body standing in it both come free · **Hoom costs six supplies**, the only free body in the game. **Gates:** `LINT()` **0** · font floor `[]` on the sheet and the battle · clip the documented `#bField`/`#bLog` · `iChar` overlap `[]` · map **0/0/0** with `f2` newly painted · no em dash, 0 doors without an intent, 1 `?` · the eight arena fights clean over three sweeps. ⚡ **AND THE GATE CAUGHT A REAL REGRESSION ON THE MERGED BUILD, WHICH IS WHAT IT IS FOR**: one `HIT GUARD` on the deserters, and a baseline diff on the same 24 fights read **4,558 ms before and 50,422 ms after**. The cause was one line in the wrong place - `surroundOn(d)` was evaluated on EVERY blow in the game instead of on the four dogs' bounce, and it reaches `engagers`, which rebuilds `alive()`. This is the 11x this file already records against putting `losState` in `hitBreakdown`, on a second surface. (b) *"keep the undo on a double move"*: the offer was real and it lasted **140ms** - `autoEnd` fires when a body has nothing left, `beginTurn` clears `B.undo` - so the one move that always empties a body was the one move that could never be taken back. The hand-over now waits while the offer is live, which is the whole change; **the turn is NOT rewound**, because order is initiative here and running `beginTurn` twice on a body would tick its morale and its poison twice.
